# CQWWCW
# Current contest: CQWWCW

# This file created by KE2D, 19 Nov. 2019, using logs from 16 top scoring stations in 2018:
# CN3A, EF8R, PJ2T, CR3DX, P33W, ES9C, PJ4Q, PJ4A, PJ4K, TI7W, KH6J, KH6LC, M6T, HQ9X, DF0HQ, 9A1A
# Contains 11,307 calls with validated or verified exchanges.
# Contains 2,693 unique calls which were found in only one of the 16 logs.
# Over 1,100 calls which could not be verified by at least one of 3 different methods have been removed.
# Total calls in this file is 14,000.
# Based on claimed score data on 3830scores.com and the official CQWW post contest scores, error rates for each log were calculated.
# Calculating the error rate became more complex as erroneous data was removed.
# However, the overall error rate is expected to be at or below 0.587% which I deemed acceptable.
# If you are working one of the 2,693 unique calls, the error probability increases slightly.
# I encourage all operators to listen carefully and copy what is sent.
# There are errors.  People move.  They operate from another QTH, etc.
# Permission is hereby granted to edit, modify, and share the file to be used with other logging programs.
# Bob Beyer - KE2D
#
